<script  type="text/javascript" data-sitespect="true">
window.__sscf.cf_10578034  = function() {
(function () {
  var POSITION = 'before-ingredients'; // of 'top'

  // --- ALLOWLIST OP SKU ---
  var ALLOW_SKUS = [
    '706636DS','706637DS','706638DS','706634DS','706635DS',
    '704700DS','704701DS','704702DS','704703DS','704704DS',
    '706618DS','706619DS','706630DS','706633DS','706631DS',
    '706632DS','706617DS'
  ];

  // --- FLAGS / HELPERS ---
  var FLAG_APPLIED = 'ssDescApplied';
  var FLAG_PLACEHOLDER_ID = 'ss-desc-placeholder';
  var FLAG_PREV_STATE = 'ssPrevState';
  function $(sel, root) { return (root || document).querySelector(sel); }
  function $all(sel, root) { return (root || document).querySelectorAll(sel); }

  // Pak SKU uit URL: /producten/<slug>-704701DS
  function getSkuFromURL() {
    var m = location.pathname.match(/-([A-Z0-9]+)$/i);
    return m ? m[1].toUpperCase() : null;
  }

  function isPDP(root) {
    return !!($('.product-detail', root) && $('[data-testid="product-description-collapsible"]', root));
  }

  function isAllowed() {
    var sku = getSkuFromURL();
    return sku ? ALLOW_SKUS.indexOf(sku) > -1 : false;
  }

  function rememberState(desc) {
    var content = desc.querySelector('.content');
    var btn = desc.querySelector('button.toggle');
    var state = {
      hadOpenClass: desc.classList.contains('open'),
      contentStyle: content ? {
        display: content.style.display,
        visibility: content.style.visibility,
        height: content.style.height
      } : null,
      btnAria: btn ? btn.getAttribute('aria-expanded') : null
    };
    desc.dataset[FLAG_PREV_STATE] = JSON.stringify(state);
  }

  function restoreState(desc) {
    var state;
    try { state = JSON.parse(desc.dataset[FLAG_PREV_STATE] || '{}'); } catch(e){}
    var content = desc.querySelector('.content');
    var btn = desc.querySelector('button.toggle');

    if (state && typeof state.hadOpenClass === 'boolean') {
      desc.classList.toggle('open', state.hadOpenClass);
    } else {
      desc.classList.remove('open');
    }

    if (btn) {
      if (state && state.btnAria != null) btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', state.btnAria);
      else btn.removeAttribute('aria-expanded');
    }
    if (content) {
      if (state && state.contentStyle) {
        content.style.display = state.contentStyle.display || '';
        content.style.visibility = state.contentStyle.visibility || '';
        content.style.height = state.contentStyle.height || '';
      } else {
        content.style.display = '';
        content.style.visibility = '';
        content.style.height = '';
      }
    }
    delete desc.dataset[FLAG_PREV_STATE];
  }

  function openCollapsible(desc) {
    if (!desc) return;
    if (!desc.classList.contains('open')) desc.classList.add('open');
    var btn = desc.querySelector('button.toggle');
    if (btn) {
      btn.removeAttribute('disabled');
      btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    }
    var content = desc.querySelector('.content');
    if (content) {
      content.style.display = '';
      content.style.visibility = 'visible';
      content.style.height = 'auto';
    }
  }

  function apply(root) {
    if (root.dataset[FLAG_APPLIED]) return;
    var container = $('.product-detail', root);
    var desc = $('[data-testid="product-description-collapsible"]', root);
    if (!container || !desc) return;

    // placeholder op originele plek
    if (!$('#' + FLAG_PLACEHOLDER_ID, root)) {
      var ph = document.createElement('span');
      ph.id = FLAG_PLACEHOLDER_ID;
      desc.parentNode.insertBefore(ph, desc);
    }

    rememberState(desc);

    if (POSITION === 'top') {
      if (container.firstElementChild !== desc) {
        container.insertBefore(desc, container.firstElementChild);
      }
    } else {
      var ingred = $('[data-testid="ingredients-collapsible"]', root);
      if (ingred && ingred.previousElementSibling !== desc) {
        container.insertBefore(desc, ingred);
      }
    }

    openCollapsible(desc);
    root.dataset[FLAG_APPLIED] = '1';
  }

  function revert(root) {
    if (!root.dataset[FLAG_APPLIED]) return;
    var desc = $('[data-testid="product-description-collapsible"]', root);
    var ph = $('#' + FLAG_PLACEHOLDER_ID, root);
    if (desc && ph && ph.parentNode) {
      ph.parentNode.insertBefore(desc, ph);
      restoreState(desc);
    }
    if (ph && ph.parentNode) ph.parentNode.removeChild(ph);
    delete root.dataset[FLAG_APPLIED];
  }

  function tick() {
    var root = document.documentElement;
    if (!isPDP(root)) { revert(root); return; }
    if (isAllowed()) apply(root);
    else revert(root);
  }

  function init() {
    tick();
    var obs = new MutationObserver(function(){ tick(); });
    obs.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });

    ['pushState','replaceState'].forEach(function (m) {
      var orig = history[m];
      history[m] = function () {
        var ret = orig.apply(this, arguments);
        setTimeout(tick, 0);
        return ret;
      };
    });
    window.addEventListener('popstate', function(){ setTimeout(tick, 0); });
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  else init();
})();
}
</script>
